# Local implementation verification and handoff

## Reading current status and dated evidence

Start with [Current coordination](PROJECT_SPEC.md#current-coordination-cadence-and-queue--october-24) and fresh Git/writer verification for completed versus active work. Each dated implementation entry preserves the state at its handoff; a later verified master integration entry/addendum supersedes its pending-integration wording. Use [recent deliveries](PROJECT_SPEC.md#october-27-local-deliveries) for contract/integration pairs and the [full index](PROJECT_SPEC.md#completed-local-deliveries) for earlier work. Historical test counts and source references do not prove current preview/hosted state or require redundant reruns. Original records and limitations remain retained below.

Reproduced and fixed through isolated browser controls:

- A cancelled/revised Prelims recording-error request left head result approval disabled even though the server correctly allowed approval. Cancelled/applied requests now stop blocking the button. The regression confirms successful actual approval, unchanged mean 10 and retained cancelled request/submissions.
- A pending Finals recording-error request left the approval button enabled until the server rejected the click. Both stage kinds now use the existing pending-correction rule. A nearby explanation and Review pending corrections button focus the exact request. Head approval alone leaves results blocked; both exact request approvals apply the amendment. The regression independently expects `24.5 / 3`, three current submissions and four retained original/amended snapshots in the operational backup.
- Archived stage review still allowed selecting vote checkboxes and offered active correction management despite the server's read-only gate. The regression confirms disabled actual controls, server rejection of cancellation, unchanged request and retained three submissions/all means 10.

Simple Place / Bib / entrant / Average columns preserve provisional versus approved status in the heading. The finalist-count warning is presented once to the head, with organizer guidance.
- Pending request cards precede resolved requests, show competition/stage, names/bibs, original and intended marks, reason and explicit approval status. Requester identities, versions/timestamps and detailed pairing records are collapsed. Both roles and exact-version actions remain independent. Organizer management stays collapsed by default; author-only submitted notes and all history remain available.
- One SubmittedReview component replaces the duplicate organizer/judge rendering paths; impossible role branches in those copies are eliminated. Revision forms remount on their exact request version to avoid displaying a prior form's defaults.
- Completed-stage judge review replaces the empty song picker/waiting message with an accurate locked-score instruction. Active song selection, pending recovery, notes and submission behavior remain. Phone result tables fit place, bib/name and average together; keyboard focus and 360/390/1440px geometry are checked.
- The public/admin CSS improves table hierarchy, row separation, readable phone typography, aligned numeric averages, expandable-section styling, focus indication and the primary event approval area. No receiver/admin logic or public data format changed. Port 3100's existing in-memory admin session was preserved without restarting the receiver.
- Frozen roster/judge/song indexes replace repeated linear searches for every submitted cell. A synthetic 200-entrant/15-judge/three-song benchmark produced identical labels for 9,000 cells: about 226 ms versus 27 ms over 40 renders. This is lookup-only evidence, not a measured full-page or hosted performance guarantee.

## Actual Finals draw directly in Run — 2026-10-09

The owner requested pairing dropdowns beside the disabled Start Finals action. Run now contains those approved J&J lead/follow rows and **Save Finals pairings** directly in its Finals card, replacing the previous navigation shortcut. A concurrent event-version change disables stale pairing edits/Save and instructs discard/review. The owner's head page reported Configure a valid finalist count even though a count had been saved. Each saved count change now increments server-owned advancementRevision in existing setup JSON and adds it to the Prelims source fingerprint, making that review distinct. Incoming revision values are ignored; ordinary saves and approval retain the revision. Older current approvals retain their fingerprints until their count changes. Earlier approval/vote/history records remain intact; no new schema/migration or frozen-stage bypass is added. The deferred replacement/redance policy is unchanged.

Entrant/couple withdrawal overlays eligibility without deleting originals, preserves past partnerships/marks, requires exact undanced J&J pairings, resets stale acknowledgement and retains every required judge even in an all-withdrawn heat.

Completed competitions prepare an exact public preview; organizer approval requests separate owner review. Server-only synthetic keys, a durable D1 outbox and a separate loopback receiver/public store implement local releases. The receiver checks the full payload fingerprint, current authorized source, scope/order, independent equal-judge means/counts/ranks and idempotent delivery. Public named judge and entrant/pair cards retain true blanks, observations and stage identities while excluding notes, participant/access identities, setup notes and private reasons. Corrections retain revision 1 Under review until independently head/organizer/owner-approved revision 2 replaces the current pointer atomically. Historical revisions remain Superseded with a current link. Suspension/shutdown retain operational judging and recovery. All result export/print/template-download/Sheets UI paths are removed; inert compatibility source and JSON recovery remain.

The full mock uses the bounded 18-Strictly-couple equivalent plus both J&J divisions, multiple heats, 2–3 songs, distinct organizer/head/two judges, separate owner and anonymous sessions. The API fixture uses 44 Strictly couples. Actual controls exercise acknowledgement cancellation/edit reset, uncovered active bibs, interrupted save/reload/focus-safe context movement, full-panel votes, approvals, explicit finalist/pair setup, immutable final correction, owner release and version navigation. Revised Finals winner equals `(4.5 + 10 + 10) / 3`, independently checked as 8.166666… rather than relying on the app to provide the expected value. Final backup has one additional immutable amendment and retains every earlier submission ID. Another unfinished competition cannot prepare a snapshot; changed post-Finals membership blocks approval/publication pending the deferred policy.

### Gaps found and resolved

- Organizer/judge data separation now covers both setup and frozen panel participant IDs; relevant judge audit uses role labels while original identities remain in operational history.
- Removing an unsaved draft competition in the older block desk no longer crashes its readiness calculation. The phone's hidden file input no longer pushes the page to 494px. Owner credential input and loading controls wait for hydration.
- Context/eligibility and ballot versions guard heat submission; withdrawn or unscheduled dancers cannot receive new marks. Current pairing plans preserve danced history and one partner per song.
- Publication verification pins the complete payload, retains status notices arriving before a release, handles source invalidation during queued delivery and guards owner service enablement atomically. J&J pair cards include both original preliminary dancers, and phone table headers remain readable.
- Reproducible compiled POST failures were traced to early authorization responses leaving bodies unread on a keep-alive connection, not actual application reloading. Five consecutive bad-credential requests initially alternated 403/503 with Workerd's “restarted mid-request” text; reading the request body before denial produced five 403 responses and a complete passing compiled suite. The receiver-only attestation endpoint uses the same lifecycle. No blanket error suppression or automatic test retries were added.
- Browser locator/delivery-readiness failures were corrected separately: explicit source-selector labels, waiting for status-delivery response, and accounting for the genuine additional immutable correction in backup history. An early acknowledgement timing failure did not reproduce in final full runs; independent revision/CAS/ack tests pass.

### Limits and local handoff

Physical phones, venue Wi-Fi, extended outages/guaranteed offline reload, hosted D1/auth/domain/quota/capacity/provisioning/migrations/deployment, production uptime/logging, real publication and actual advance shutdown notice remain unperformed. The maintained operational tool / 30-day notice where practicable are product policies, not measured hosting guarantees. No deployment, provisioned service, real external result release or purchase occurred. The post-Finals changed-membership replacement/redance policy, reinstatement/substitutes and legacy-only publication remain deliberately deferred.

Preview remains `http://127.0.0.1:3000`; separate synthetic public site is `http://127.0.0.1:3100`; `/owner` requires the ignored local owner credential and is separate from event roles. The local dispatcher retries existing authorized deliveries/statuses and creates no approvals. Compiled test processes are stopped after persistence checks; their stores remain ignored. Screenshots/restart evidence and synthetic private fixtures are ignored under `work/verification-20261009`, `work/` and `test-results/`.

PROJECT_SPEC.md now contains implemented behavior/timeline, branding, confirmed policies, acceptance matrix and complete isolated mock. AGENTS.md routes to the three permanent references. The temporary NEXT_BATCH_SPEC.md is removed after folding; Git retains its history. Affected source/doc/new migration/asset files were compared against the baseline and selectively synced to the Documents mirror, excluding Git metadata, node_modules, secrets, generated outputs and D1 stores. The application branch retains the local uncommitted batch for review.

A first compiled-Worker run failed while sharing SQLite files with the running development server. Running that Worker with a separate ignored synthetic store eliminated the contention; the preview’s database was not reset. Development/browser and compiled tests now have separate local storage. This was an environment isolation issue, not a reason to suppress storage errors or relax judging requirements.

## Complete synthetic rehearsal

API fixture: 44 Strictly couples across both divisions, three heats per division, 2–3 songs per heat; two J&J divisions with six individual entrants and rotating partners; three judges including a separate head and a distinct organizer. The full browser fixture uses 18 Strictly couples plus both J&J divisions to keep real button interaction bounded. Expected numeric/voting outcomes are independently specified.

| Step | Verified behavior / evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Setup and preparation | Equivalent reused setup UI in local/shared modes; add/import entrants, add/remove heats, cancel/confirm removal, unassigned roster recovery, unique heat names, Save setup/reload, remove competition, select a survivor, remove the last competition and re-add. Surviving legacy scores/revisions and removed-history backups remain intact. Ordinary local template edits create no server resources; explicit role-link buttons now create/reopen a local D1 rehearsal. |
| Access and private links | Distinct participant identifiers and attestation required before a stage; overlaps/head exclusion rejected. Organizer cannot score/vote/approve results. Revocation/replacement/open controls act on real synthetic links; cancelled revocation leaves access intact, revoked access fails and replacement works. Event-long links survive advancement/setup changes. |
| Prelim heats and song pages | Every scheduled division/heat/song runs in order. Visited song pages keep separate marks/notes and rotation context. No mid-stage averages/cards are exposed, including through organizer/head API reads. Future songs cannot be selected. |
| Coverage and lineup | Deliberately uncovered bib is identified and blocks submission; note alone does not satisfy coverage. No is scored; individual song blanks remain explicit. A genuine observed mark during lineup resolves coverage. |
| Final heat acknowledgement | Checked-bibs/marks acknowledgement plus a named final confirmation. Cancellation produces no official snapshot and leaves editing available. Editing clears acknowledgement. Server rejects absent acknowledgement or stale reviewed ballot versions; immutable snapshots record acknowledgement time. Acknowledged heat entries disappear and the next assigned heat becomes available. |
| Save recovery and races | Interrupted save, online reload/retry, note focus/scroll during room changes, private-note isolation, duplicate operation/submission retries, competing cell revisions and a save racing heat submission. One race winner commits; late writes to submitted/withdrawn work are rejected. Delayed reads cannot restore finalized forms. |
| Whole-stage review | Every required submission gates room advancement/conclusion. Submitted cards are read only, all blanks remain visible, counts distinguish observations from judge/heat totals, and judge song means are weighted equally. Explicit independent example: means 10, 5, 5 yield 20/3 with counts 1, 2, 2. |
| Genuine mistaken-bib correction | Request identifies wrong/intended cells and reason. Organizer approval alone changes no mark; revised request clears its approvals; an old version is rejected. Both distinct approvals apply an immutable amendment, retaining original/corrected values and timestamps. Numeric expected results are 10 for bib 101 and 4.5 for bib 102 in the full fixture. |
| Advancement votes and renewed review | All panel members vote exactly the remaining places. Organizer cannot vote; partial quorum blocks approval. Majority chooses advancement separately from numeric ranks. Three-way boundary deadlock requires the actual head button; certain higher-vote candidates remain selected. A correction of already-approved prelims invalidates prior votes/head decisions/approval and requires new review. |
| Finals | Exact currently approved finalists required; manual fixed J&J pairs retained through all final songs. All final heats use the same coverage/acknowledgement locks. Ordinary roles cannot decide finals ties. Actual head buttons resolve tied 4.5 averages into places 1/2 while both averages stay 4.5. Full fixture winners retain expected mean 10. |
| Results and exports | Provisional/approved labels, observation counts, current own vote choices and separate head decisions. Actual CSV, scorecard CSV, amendment JSON and backup downloads contain blanks/history and exclude default notes/access codes. Print switches to Results and invokes a test print handler. Optional Sheets request is intercepted locally and validates the existing rectangular row contract; no Google request leaves the test. |
| Withdrawal and restore | Actual request/cancel/approve controls require both distinct roles; all submitted records/earlier approvals survive in history. Withdrawing the current last division leaves a usable remaining-stage conclusion. Shared restore creates new links/preparation state with inert history. Legacy wrapped/plain v2 backups reload without score/revision conversion. |

## Reproducible gaps fixed

- Missing local competition removal and unsafe assumptions about the last competition.
- Rich local setup becoming stale when using retained legacy metadata/roster controls.
- Explicit Save setup reporting completion before its debounced storage write; immediate reload now retains it.
- Navigation clicks being lost during initial hydration/connection.
- Future song selection and completed-heat metadata rolling back on delayed reads.
- Withdrawal of the current last competition stranding the remaining stage.
- Started organizer/attestation identities being editable; excluding head from a required stage panel.
- Result captions calling heat snapshots “judges”, calling approved preliminary equal averages “tie pending”, and hiding the author’s persisted vote choices after reload.

No `send was called before connect` application/dev-client rejection appeared in the passing isolated browser runs. No blanket rejection handler was introduced. Initial test-locator/readiness failures were corrected separately from the reproducible application gaps.

## Remaining decision and unperformed checks

If a genuine correction after Finals has already danced changes who should have advanced, the app invalidates approvals and blocks approval of mismatched Finals. The replacement-finalist/redance policy remains undecided; no automatic replacement or redance is implemented. The rehearsal corrects before advancement and separately tests invalidation of already-approved advancement.

Physical phones, real venue Wi-Fi, extended outages/guaranteed offline reload, hosted D1 allowances/provisioning/migrations/deployment, production logging/capacity and real Google Apps Script delivery remain unperformed. Local rehearsal organizer/judge clipboard contents were verified in isolated Edge. A native printer/dialog was not exercised; print invocation was simulated. Identifier attestation cannot prove which human physically holds a link. Neither mode has a service worker or an offline reload guarantee. Retained legacy backup validation/save recovery limitations remain documented in PROJECT_SPEC.md.

Synthetic screenshot artifacts are ignored under `test-results/`, with preserved copies in workspace `work/`. The existing local preview stays available on `http://127.0.0.1:3000`. Test artifacts and private synthetic link codes are not part of the source change.
